### [120] [Composite Fields and Tree Expansions: A Unified Framework for Renormalized Vertex Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06449)
*Oleksandr Sulyma,Benedikt Schneider*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于复合场有效作用量Legendre变换的泛函框架，该框架在代数层面重新组织了量子场论中顶点函数的图解内容，将parquet、单玻色子交换、渐近类和对称估计等已知顶点分解统一为同一泛函结构的不同实现，并可借助树展开将高阶顶点系统性地推广到四点半顶点之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中的单粒子不可约顶点函数虽然编码了重整化相互作用，但其高维数以及对外部变量（如动量或频率）的复杂依赖使其实际处理极具挑战性。现有的多种顶点分解方案（parquet、单玻色子交换等）各自独立发展，缺乏统一的数学结构来揭示它们之间的深层联系，并难以系统地推广到更高阶顶点。

Method: 基于复合场的有效作用量的Legendre变换构建泛函框架，通过选择不同的复合自由度，推导出不同的顶点表示。该方法在代数层面重组顶点函数的图解内容为更高效的构造块，并通过树展开系统地生成高阶顶点。

Result: 该框架证明了parquet分解、单玻色子交换、渐近类和对称估计等已知顶点分解均可作为同一泛函结构的不同实现；通过树展开可将这些分解推广到四点以外的高阶顶点；且该框架不依赖任何特定物理体系，广泛适用于凝聚态物理和粒子物理中的量子场论。

Conclusion: 本文建立了统一描述不同顶点分解方案的泛函框架，揭示了它们共同的数学本质——即复合自由度选择的不同导致顶点表示形式的不同，并通过树展开实现了高于四点的系统性推广，为量子场论中的顶点函数处理提供了普适且高效的理论工具。

Abstract: One-particle irreducible vertices encode renormalized interactions in quantum field theories, but their practical treatment remains challenging due to their high dimensionality and nontrivial dependence on external variables such as momenta or frequencies. We develop a functional framework that algebraically reorganizes the diagrammatic content of the vertex functions into more efficient building blocks and reveals several established vertex decompositions, such as parquet, single-boson exchange, asymptotic classes and symmetric estimators, as different realizations of a common functional structure. Based on Legendre transforms of the effective action of composite fields, the formalism shows that different vertex representations arise from different choices of composite degrees of freedom. We demonstrate that in our framework higher-order vertices can be systematically obtained via tree expansions, thereby extending the aforementioned decompositions beyond the four-point level. Because our findings are independent of any specific physical setting, the framework applies broadly to quantum field theories in condensed matter physics, particle physics, and beyond.

</details>


### [121] [Excitonic Magnetism in Ruthenium Pyrochlores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06504)
*Swetlana Swarup,Yang Yang,Natalia B. Perkins*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文发展了一套适用于烧绿石晶格上Ru⁴⁺离子的Van Vleck激子磁性微观理论，从多轨道Hubbard模型出发推导出单重态-三重态流形内的有效超交换相互作用，通过triplon激发谱和变分方法分析激子哈密顿量，揭示非磁J=0单重态的triplon凝聚不稳定性，绘制了磁性相图并应用于Nd₂Ru₂O₇等烧绿石钌酸盐，发现其接近激子量子临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: d⁴体系中强自旋轨道耦合预期稳定非磁J=0单重态基态，但许多烧绿石钌酸盐却表现出稳健的长程磁有序，这一表面矛盾亟需理论解释，促使作者建立微观理论框架来协调二者。

Method: 从包含自旋轨道耦合的多轨道Hubbard模型出发，推导Ru⁴⁺离子低能单重态-三重态流形内的有效超交换相互作用；利用triplon激发谱和凝聚相的变分处理两种方法分析激子哈密顿量，识别单重态失稳条件并确定作为微观跳迁参数函数的磁性相图。

Result: 相图复现了常规烧绿石模型中已知的磁有序态，同时预测了一个仅在单重态-三重态描述中出现的额外磁相。将理论应用于Nd₂Ru₂O₇等烧绿石钌酸盐，表明其处于接近激子量子临界点的位置。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了理解烧绿石钌酸盐中激子磁性及其磁激发谱的微观框架，为Raman散射等光谱探测提供了直接的理论联系。

Abstract: Strong spin-orbit coupling in $d^4$ systems is expected to stabilize a nonmagnetic $J=0$ singlet ground state, yet many ruthenium pyrochlores exhibit robust long-range magnetic order. Motivated by this apparent contradiction, we develop a microscopic theory of Van Vleck excitonic magnetism on the pyrochlore lattice. Starting from a multi-orbital Hubbard model with spin-orbit coupling, we derive the effective superexchange interactions within the low-energy singlet--triplet manifold of Ru$^{4+}$ ions. We analyze the resulting excitonic Hamiltonian using both the spectrum of triplon excitations and a variational treatment of the condensed phase. We identify the instability of the nonmagnetic singlet state toward triplon condensation and determine the resulting magnetic phase diagram as a function of the microscopic hopping parameters. The phase diagram reproduces the magnetic orders known from conventional pyrochlore models while also predicting an additional magnetic phase unique to the singlet--triplet description. Finally, we apply the theory to the pyrochlore ruthenates, with particular emphasis on Nd$_2$Ru$_2$O$_7$, and show that it lies in close proximity to the excitonic quantum critical point. Our results establish a microscopic framework for understanding excitonic magnetism in pyrochlore ruthenates and their magnetic excitation spectrum, providing direct connections to spectroscopic probes, including Raman scattering.

</details>


### [122] [Suppression of orbital ordering and emergence of a glassy magnetic state in a high-entropy spinel vanadate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06738)
*Shun Ito,Sota Nakakuki,Satoshi Demura,Tadataka Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种高熵尖晶石钒酸盐(Li0.2Mg0.2Mn0.2Co0.2Zn0.2)V2O4的结构、热力学与磁性。该化合物在低至5 K时保持立方尖晶石结构不变，抑制了长程轨道有序，表现出~20 K以下的玻璃态磁冻结和异质磁弛豫动力学，介于典型自旋玻璃与团簇玻璃之间。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵氧化物为研究构型无序与关联电子态的相互作用提供了独特平台，但此前缺乏对高熵尖晶石钒酸盐中阻挫磁性与轨道自由度相互作用的系统研究。

Method: 采用X射线衍射、磁化率测量、比热测量、交流磁化率（频率依赖）以及基于Mydosh参数、动态标度律、Vogel-Fulcher律和Cole-Cole分析的动力学分析。

Result: 样品在5 K以下保持立方尖晶石结构，无对称性降低；磁化率显示~20 K以下玻璃态冻结，比热在3 K以下无长程有序异常；交流磁化率显示显著玻璃态动力学，介于正则自旋玻璃与团簇玻璃之间；Cole-Cole分析揭示偏离单一Debye弛豫，存在异质磁弛豫。

Conclusion: 高构型熵抑制了长程轨道有序并稳定了异质玻璃态磁性，凸显了几何阻挫、轨道自由度和构型无序在高熵磁体中的协同作用。

Abstract: High-entropy oxides provide a unique platform for exploring the interplay between configurational disorder and correlated electronic states. We report on structural, thermodynamic, and magnetic properties of the high-entropy spinel vanadate (Li$_{0.2}$Mg$_{0.2}$Mn$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Zn$_{0.2}$)V$_2$O$_4$, in which orbital-active V ions occupy the pyrochlore sublattice. X-ray diffraction measurements reveal that the cubic spinel structure is preserved at temperatures down to 5 K without any detectable symmetry lowering. Unlike conventional spinel vanadates exhibiting a symmetry-lowering structural transition driven by orbital ordering, the compound that we study exhibits a suppression of long-range orbital ordering in the high-entropy state. Magnetic susceptibility measurements show glassy magnetic freezing at temperatures below $\sim$20 K, while specific-heat measurements reveal no anomaly associated with long-range ordering down to 3 K. Frequency-dependent ac susceptibility reveals pronounced glassy dynamics. Analyses based on the Mydosh parameter, dynamic scaling law, and Vogel--Fulcher law suggest an intermediate dynamical regime between canonical spin-glass and cluster-glass behavior. Furthermore, Cole--Cole analyses reveal systematic deviations from a single Debye relaxation process, indicating the presence of heterogeneous magnetic relaxation dynamics near the freezing regime. Our results demonstrate that, in a frustrated spinel vanadate, high configurational entropy suppresses long-range orbital ordering and stabilizes a heterogeneous glassy magnetic state, highlighting the combined roles of geometrical frustration, orbital degrees of freedom, and configurational disorder in high-entropy magnets.

</details>


### [123] [Prominent Intrinsic Orbital Hall Effect in the 135 Kagome Metal Family and Orbital Responses in the Loop-Current Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06775)
*Jianxin Huang,Rina Tazai,Youichi Yamakawa,Hiroshi Kontani*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了过渡金属kagome晶格金属中的轨道霍尔效应(OHE)，提出CsTi₃Bi₅中具有大的正OHE，而CsV₃Sb₅和CsCr₃Sb₅中具有负OHE。通过轨道扇区分解发现|lz|=2的d轨道产生正贡献，而|lz|=1的d和p轨道产生负贡献，强p-d杂化调控各轨道扇区的定量平衡进而决定总OHE符号。另外研究了CsV₃Sb₅的环路电流相，发现其诱导出有限的局域原子轨道角动量，且相关对称性降低允许轨道电导率张量存在有限对称分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子轨道自由度（包括轨道电流和轨道角动量的产生与控制）已成为活跃的研究领域。轨道霍尔效应是轨道电流产生的关键机制之一，理解其在过渡金属kagome晶格金属中的表现，对于探索强关联kagome金属中的轨道电流和局域轨道角动量响应具有重要意义。

Method: 采用理论模型计算的方法，对CsTi₃Bi₅、CsV₃Sb₅和CsCr₃Sb₅模型进行OHE研究；通过轨道扇区分解（orbital-sector decomposition）区分不同|lz|值轨道的贡献；通过控制变量计算分析轨道扇区符号趋势和p-d杂化对总OHE符号的调控；进一步对CsV₃Sb₅的环路电流相进行对称性分析和轨道电导率张量计算。

Result: 发现CsTi₃Bi₅具有大的正OHE，CsV₃Sb₅和CsCr₃Sb₅具有负OHE；|lz|=2的d轨道通道产生正贡献，|lz|=1的d和p轨道通道产生负贡献；轨道扇区符号趋势在接近实际填充时保持稳健，而强p-d杂化调控定量平衡并决定总OHE符号；CsV₃Sb₅的环路电流相诱导有限局域原子轨道角动量，且对称性降低允许轨道电导率张量的有限对称分量存在。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了kagome金属中OHE的化合物依赖性和填充依赖性同时反映各轨道扇区的符号趋势以及杂化控制的平衡关系，为在强关联kagome金属中探索轨道电流和局域轨道角动量响应提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The study of electronic orbital degrees of freedom, including the generation and control of orbital currents and orbital angular momentum, has emerged as a vibrant research field. Here, we study the orbital Hall effect (OHE), one of the key mechanisms for orbital current generation, in transition-metal kagome-lattice metals. We propose a large positive OHE in CsTi$_3$Bi$_5$ and negative OHEs in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ and CsCr$_3$Sb$_5$ models. Orbital-sector decomposition shows that the $\vert l^z_d \vert=2$ $d$-orbital channel gives a positive contribution, whereas the $\vert l^z \vert=1$ $d$- and $p$-orbital channels can give negative contributions. Control calculations suggest a persistent orbital-sector sign tendency near the actual filling, while strong $p$-$d$ hybridization modulates the quantitative balance and total OHE sign. Thus, the compound and filling dependence of the OHE reflects both the sign tendency of each orbital sector and the hybridization-controlled balance among them. Furthermore, we investigate the loop-current phase of CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ and show that it induces finite local atomic orbital angular momentum. We also show that loop-current-related symmetry lowering allows finite symmetric components of the orbital conductivity tensor. This study provides a basis for exploring orbital currents and local orbital-angular-momentum responses in strongly correlated kagome metals.

</details>


### [124] [Local magnetic properties of the rare-earth intermetallics $R$Mn$_2$Ge$_2$ ($R$=Ce, Pr, Nd)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07013)
*N. P. Bentley,T. L. Breeze,A. Hernández-Melián,M. J. Pearce,T. J. Hicken,M. T. F. Telling,B. M. Huddart,D. A. Mayoh,G. Balakrishnan,S. J. Clark,T. Lancaster*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种稀土金属间化合物RMn₂Ge₂（R=Ce, Pr, Nd）的磁性，发现在从锥形磁结构向共线反铁磁结构转变的温度区间内存在磁局域涨落，其动力学特征与已知的skyrmion宿主材料相似，且该转变以上的无序程度沿Ce到Nd的序列递增；低温下磁性受稀土离子有序化影响而呈现不同行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些材料被报道在室温下承载skyrmionic气泡晶格，但对其磁性机理及调控因素的理解尚不充分。通过系统对比同一系列不同稀土元素的磁性行为，可以揭示skyrmion气泡形成的物理机制及无序度、稀土离子有序化等因素的作用。

Method: 对RMn₂Ge₂（R=Ce, Pr, Nd）三种材料进行系统的磁性表征，追踪从锥形磁结构到共线反铁磁结构的温度演化过程，分析局域涨落动力学以及不同稀土元素对整个系列的调控效应。

Result: 研究发现三种材料在skyrmion气泡报道的温度区间内展示出与已知skyrmion宿主材料相似的动力学特征；无序程度在该转变温度以上沿R=Ce到Nd序列递增；低温下稀土离子有序化导致各成员表现出不同的磁性行为。

Conclusion: RMn₂Ge₂系列中skyrmion气泡的形成与从锥形磁结构到共线反铁磁结构的转变过程中的局域涨落密切相关，无序度和稀土离子有序化是调控该系列材料低温磁性的关键因素。

Abstract: We present an investigation of the rare-earth intermetallic materials, $R$Mn$_2$Ge$_2$ ($R$=Ce, Pr, Nd), reported to host a lattice of skyrmionic bubbles at room temperature. The magnetism of all three materials is characterised by the onset of local fluctuations as the magnetic state changes from a conical magnetic structure to a collinear antiferromagnetic one as temperature $T$ is increased. In this $T$ regime, where skyrmion-bubble textures have been reported, we see dynamics similar to those observed in other skyrmion-hosting materials. The level of disorder above this transition increases across the series from $R$=Ce to Nd. At low temperatures the magnetism is affected by the ordering of the rare-earth ions, resulting in distinct behaviour for the different members of the series.

</details>


### [125] [Entwined lattice of atoms and anionic electrons in layered electride LaCl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07322)
*Songyuan Geng,Xin Wang,Jianqi Zhong,Risi Guo,Fangjie Chen,Qun Wang,Kangjie Li,Keyu An,Teng-Fei Ying,Chen Qiu,Hanpu Liang,Zhengtai Liu,Mao Ye,Sungsoo Hahn,Balasubramanian Thiagarajan,Benjamin T. Zhou,Haoxiang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）实验和紧束缚模型分析，发现LaCl中的阴离子电子晶格（AEL）与La阳离子框架发生耦合，与YCl中孤立的AEL形成鲜明对比，导致电子结构从二分dice晶格网络重构为三分结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电化物（electrides）中的多余电子可以组织成阴离子电子晶格（AEL），提供了类似晶格几何的设计自由度。然而，AEL与原子晶格之间的耦合如何影响电子结构尚未被充分探索。

Method: 使用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）对LaCl进行实验测量，结合紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）分析，并与同构的YCl进行对比。

Result: LaCl中的AEL与La阳离子框架耦合，激活了AEL与La原子晶格之间的直接跳跃通道，导致电子结构完全重构；其Chern带拓扑发生改变，将YCl中的二分dice晶格网络转变为LaCl中的三分结构。

Conclusion: AEL与原子晶格之间的耦合可以主动塑造控制电子结构的有效晶格几何，这一耦合可作为传统材料中无法实现的电子结构设计的有力调节手段。

Abstract: Controlling the lattice geometry that governs electronic structure is a central theme in condensed-matter physics, yet in crystalline solids this geometry is usually fixed by the atomic framework. Electrides offer an alternative route to electronic structure design in which their excess electrons can organize into anionic electron lattice (AEL) and provide a lattice-like degree of freedom. Recent work has highlighted the standalone limit, where the AEL in YCl yields bands well described by the dice-lattice model. Here, using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), we show that LaCl, although isostructural to YCl, realizes a qualitatively different regime where the AEL is entwined with the La cation framework, producing a fully reconstructed electronic structure. Combining the ARPES result with tight-binding model analysis, we demonstrate that this radical divergence stems from the activation of direct hopping channels between the AEL and the La atomic lattice. This coupling reshapes the effective lattice geometry, reconstructs the electronic states, and modifies the associated Chern band topology, transforming the bipartite dice-lattice network in YCl into a tripartite structure in LaCl. Our findings demonstrate that the coupling between the AEL and the atomic lattice can actively shape the effective lattice geometry that governs the electronic structure. This coupling can act as a powerful tuning knob for electronic structure design that is inaccessible in conventional materials.

</details>


### [126] [A quantum geometric mechanism for chiral domain wall metastability: Application to twisted transition-metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07443)
*Nisarg Chadha,Qiang Gao,Eslam Khalaf,Zhaoyu Han*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究Chern共轭能带（时间反演相关）铁磁体中的拓扑激发行为。尽管净电荷响应为零，光滑自旋织构可携带偶极响应——沿长度方向具有面内缠绕的畴壁可束缚横向的非零偶极密度，其强度由几何偶极系数c_G控制。该系数是混合（动量-序参量）空间中占据投影算符第二Chern形式的矩，一般非零但不量子化。偶极装饰的畴壁可在有限半径处因偶极排斥与表面张力竞争而成为亚稳态。在扭曲MoTe2模型中，c_G在谷极化（VP）相内部C=1到C=0的转变处急剧下降，解释了最近的泵浦-探测实验中长寿命激发在超过饱和场的反向场下仍存活、但在中间位移场处消失的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对能带拓扑在铁磁激发中印记的研究集中于量子霍尔铁磁体，其中相同Chern数两味态的光滑skyrmion织构束缚电荷。本文旨在探索Chern共轭能带（时间反演相关的两味态）情形——此时净电荷响应为零，但可能存在新的拓扑响应通道（偶极响应），并解释扭曲莫尔铁磁体中的反常实验现象。

Method: 理论推导上，作者证明在Chern共轭能带中光滑织构可产生偶极响应：具有面内缠绕的畴壁可束缚横向非零偶极密度。通过推导出偶极密度由无量纲系数c_G控制，该系数是混合（动量-序参量）空间中占据投影算符第二Chern形式的矩。在真实物理上，作者将该理论应用于扭曲MoTe2模型，计算c_G随位移场的变化，并与C=1到C=0的磁相变及泵浦-探测实验结果进行对照。

Result: 发现c_G虽然是几何量且未被量子化，但在扭曲MoTe2中VP相内部C=1→C=0转变处急剧下降。这一行为自然解释了在空穴填充ν=1时观测到的泵浦-探测实验：长寿命激发能在超过饱和场的反向场下存活，但会在中等位移场处消失，即使传统磁诊断仅显示微弱变化。

Conclusion: 亚稳态自旋织构可作为能带量子几何的灵敏探针，同时也是Chern共轭能带中moiré铁磁体快速光学控制的内在瓶颈。偶极响应为拓扑响应提供了一种净电荷之外的通道，拓宽了能带拓扑对磁激发印记的理解。

Abstract: Band topology can have an imprint on the excitations of a ferromagnet. A known example is quantum Hall ferromagnets and their lattice analogs; when both flavors have the same Chern number $C$, a smooth skyrmion texture binds charge $-eC$ per unit winding. Here, we consider instead the case of conjugate Chern bands related by time-reversal. We show that, despite the vanishing net charge response, a smooth texture can be associated with a dipole response---a domain wall (DW) with an in-plane winding along its length can bind a nonzero dipole density transverse to the wall. The strength of this dipole density is controlled by a dimensionless coefficient $c_G$. Although not quantized, the geometric dipole coefficient $c_G$ is a moment of the second Chern form of the occupied projector in mixed (momentum and order-parameter) space and is generally nonzero. The dipole-decorated DW can thus become metastable at a finite radius due to the competition between dipolar repulsion and the usual surface tension, even at a finite Zeeman field. In a realistic model of twisted MoTe$_2$, we find that $c_G$ drops sharply across a transition within the valley-polarized (VP) phase from a $C=1$ to a $C=0$ ferromagnet. This naturally explains recent pump-probe experiments~\cite{exp} at hole filling $ν=1$, in which a long-lived excitation survives reverse fields far exceeding the saturation field but disappears at an intermediate displacement field despite only weak changes in conventional magnetic diagnostics. Metastable spin textures thus serve as a sensitive probe of band quantum geometry, and as an intrinsic bottleneck for fast optical control of moiré ferromagnets in Chern-conjugate bands.

</details>

### [127] [A combinatorial rule for $GL$-multiplicities of $A_n$-quiver loci](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06664)
*Ian Cavey,Andrew Hardt,Alexander Yong*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文首次给出了A型箭图轨道闭包的坐标环中不可约GL表示重数的正面组合规则，适用于箭图的所有定向。在此之前，仅有De Concini-Strickland在20世纪80年代初对复形簇这一特殊情形给出了隐式描述。论文中的组合对象带有晶体结构，其最高权元素用于计算这些重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为A型箭图轨道闭包的坐标环中不可约GL表示重数提供正面组合规则，填补此前仅对复形簇特殊情形有隐式描述、缺乏一般情形的显式组合方法的空白。

Method: 构造带有晶体结构的组合对象，通过该晶体结构中的最高权元素计算多重集的基数，从而得到不可约表示的重数组合规则。

Result: 给出了适用于箭图所有定向的正面组合规则，推广了De Concini-Strickland仅对复形簇的隐式描述结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为A型箭图轨道闭包的表示论重数问题提供了统一的、面向所有定向的组合刻画，且组合对象上晶体结构的存在暗示了更深层的表示论结构。

Abstract: We give the first positive combinatorial rule for the multiplicities of irreducible $GL$-representations in the coordinate rings of type $A$ quiver orbit closures, valid for every orientation of the quiver. Previously, for the special case of varieties of complexes, work of De Concini--Strickland in the early 1980s gave an implicit description of these multiplicities. The combinatorial objects in our rule carry a crystal structure whose highest-weight elements compute these multiplicities.

</details>


### [128] [Relative Interval Tilting, Higher Auslander Staircase Corners and Rational Dyck Posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06696)
*Shengyong Pan*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文构造了有理Dyck阶梯的关联代数与型A高阶Auslander代数的幂等角之间的显式倾斜等价。在互素情形下，该角与Xing引入的代数B_0一致，从而证明了Chapoton-Ladkani-Rognerud猜想。该等价不需要互素性假设，并兼容复制代数。主要工具是区间倾斜机制的线性范畴推广，允许任意有限维Hom空间和非零态射的零复合。迭代构造给出有限坐标阶梯的关联代数与高阶Auslander代数幂等角之间的导出等价，并在Fukaya范畴和Fukaya-Seidel范畴中实现了相应的导出范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有关于Dyck阶梯与Auslander代数之间等价的链条，但缺少关键一环，即Chapoton-Ladkani-Rognerud猜想尚未被证明。作者旨在填补这一缺口，并将结果推广到更一般的有限\kk-线性范畴框架以及更广的阶梯类型。

Method: 将Chapoton-Ladkani-Rognerud的区间倾斜机制推广到线性范畴层面，在总范畴及其纤维满足有限整体维数假设的条件下，利用精确右Kan延拓构造倾斜对象；通过逐坐标迭代构造，获得有限坐标阶梯关联代数与高阶Auslander代数幂等角之间的显式导出等价。

Result: 证明了互素正整数情形的Chapoton-Ladkani-Rognerud猜想；建立了无需互素假设的Dyck-角等价；将所得阶梯导出范畴实现为部分缠绕Fukaya范畴中的三角子范畴，以及在互素Dyck情形下实现为对称Brieskorn-Pham奇点的Fukaya-Seidel范畴。

Conclusion: 本文通过线性范畴的倾斜机制统一并推广了此前的区间倾斜理论，证明了关键猜想，并将代数结果与辛几何（Fukaya范畴）建立了联系，揭示了Dyck阶梯代数与高阶Auslander代数及镜像对称之间的深层结构关系。

Abstract: We construct an explicit tilting equivalence between the incidence algebra of every rational Dyck staircase and a canonical idempotent corner of a higher Auslander algebra of type~$A$. In the coprime case, this corner identifies with the algebra $B_0$ introduced by Xing. The resulting Dyck-corner equivalence supplies the missing link in the previously known chain of equivalences and thereby proves the Chapoton--Ladkani--Rognerud conjecture for coprime positive integers. The Dyck-corner equivalence itself requires no coprimality hypothesis and is compatible with replicated algebras.
  Our main tool is a linear-categorical extension of the interval-tilting mechanism of Chapoton--Ladkani--Rognerud. The relative theorem applies to finite $\kk$-linear categories under finite-global-dimension assumptions on the total category and its fibers. In contrast with the incidence-category setting, it allows arbitrary finite-dimensional $\Hom$ spaces and zero composites of nonzero morphisms, and it does not require the diagonal endomorphism algebras to be semisimple. The tilting object is constructed from exact right Kan extensions of fiberwise representables. We compute its opposite indexed endomorphism category, including all forced-zero compositions, and hence its opposite endomorphism algebra. Iterating this construction one coordinate at a time yields an explicit derived equivalence between the incidence algebra of every finite coordinate staircase and an idempotent corner of a higher Auslander algebra of type~$A$. We further realize the resulting staircase derived categories as triangulated subcategories generated by product Lagrangians in partially wrapped Fukaya categories of stopped-disk symmetric products and, in the coprime Dyck case, as Fukaya--Seidel categories of symmetric Brieskorn--Pham singularities.

</details>


### [129] [Decomposition matrices of cyclotomic $q$-web categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07394)
*Linliang Song,Weiqiang Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文发展了循环q-网（cyclotomic q-webs）的内自同态代数的胞腔结构。这些代数构成一类新的量子代数族，介于循环Hecke代数和循环q-Schur代数之间。作者证明了在q为一般位置或单位根时，这些代数的模范畴的Grothendieck群同构于量子仿射gl_p的可积最高权模；进一步地，该同构将投射不可分解模的类映射到典范基。这一结果大幅推广了Lascoux-Leclerc-Thibon、Ariki和Varagnolo-Vasserot的经典工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环q-网的内自同态代数是一个新引入的量子代数族，其地位介于循环Hecke代数与循环q-Schur代数之间。作者的目标是建立这类新代数的表示论结构，特别是其模范畴的Grothendieck群与量子仿射李代数可积表示之间的联系，从而在更广泛的框架下统一和推广已有的经典结果。理解这类代数的胞腔结构是揭示其表示论性质的关键步骤。

Method: 作者首先为循环q-网的内自同态代数构造了胞腔结构（cellular structures），这是理解其表示论的基础。随后利用这种胞腔结构分析模范畴，分别处理q为一般位置和q为单位根两种情形。在q为单位根的情形下，通过分析模范畴的Grothendieck群并追踪投射不可分解模的类，建立了到量子仿射gl_p可积最高权模的同构，并验证该同构将投射不可分解模的像对应到典范基。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 建立了循环q-网内自同态代数的胞腔结构；(2) 证明了在q为一般位置或单位根时，其模范畴的Grothendieck群同构于量子仿射gl_p的某个可积最高权模；(3) 证明了投射不可分解模的类在典范基下与经典基元素一一对应。这实质上推广了LLT猜想、Ariki定理以及Varagnolo-Vasserot定理。

Conclusion: 本文为循环q-网的内自同态代数建立了完整的胞腔理论和表示论框架，将其Grothendieck群与量子仿射gl_p的可积表示联系起来，并将投射不可分解模映射到典范基。该结果将LLT、Ariki和Varagnolo-Vasserot的经典工作统一并推广到更广的量子代数族上，为后续研究这些介于Hecke与q-Schur代数之间的中间量子代数提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the cellular structures for the endomorphism algebras of cyclotomic $q$-webs, which form a new family of quantum algebras sitting in between cyclotomic Hecke algebras and cyclotomic $q$-Schur algebras. We show that the Grothendieck group of a module category of the cyclotomic $q$-webs for $q$ generic or a root of unity is isomorphic to an integrable highest weight module over quantum affine $\mathfrak{gl}_p$; moreover, the isomorphism maps the classes of projective indecomposable modules to the canonical basis. This substantially generalizes the classic works of Lascoux-Leclerc-Thibon, Ariki, and Varagnolo-Vasserot.

</details>

### [130] [The Reeb Structure of Bend Distance in Grid Domains: Cycle Bounds with Holes, Exact Sector Geometry on Disks, and the Two-Port Constant $c_2^\square=3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06421)
*Aoji Li,Guangbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散化简单多边形上协调运动规划的固定参数算法所依赖的单端口扇区分解的结构理论，将其推广到带孔网格域，证明了扇区图的第一贝蒂数不超过孔数h，并在线性时间内构造出不超过h个扇区顶点的反馈集；同时指出端点半精化约定对保持单端口树引理的必要性，且未获得f(k,h)n^{O(1)}算法，仅提供结构第一步和证伪工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（ICALP 2026）中的固定参数算法建立在单端口扇区分解之上，但该分解仅针对无孔简单多边形。需要将其推广到带孔网格域以扩展应用范围，同时验证其端点约定精确性对理论正确性的影响。

Method: 将扇区分解发展为一套结构理论：明确端点约定后，弯曲距离自然延拓为定义在域上的典范分片仿射函数；扇区图刻画为该函数Reeb多重图的平行边投影。对有限纯平面立方域（含h个孔），证明扇区图第一贝蒂数≤Reeb多重图的第一贝蒂数≤h，并给出线性时间反馈集构造算法；借助坐标级单孔例子展示无孔情形下的唯一前驱与直基线性质在带孔情形失效；对无孔立方碟证明其双端口公共细化树宽恰为3。

Result: 建立了带孔网格域上扇区分解的完整结构理论：贝蒂数上界为h，反馈集至多h个顶点；无孔情形下机械完全精确，双端口公共细化树宽恰为3；端点半精化必要——按字面定义一个无孔单位正方形会出现C3扇区图从而推翻单端口树引理，而增强约定恰好等价于原文分层程序所计算的度量；最终未获得f(k,h)n^{O(1)}算法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了带孔域上扇区分解的结构性基础与对该固定参数算法程序的反证工具，但算法复杂性目标尚未达成，需进一步研究。

Abstract: The fixed-parameter algorithm for coordinated motion planning on discretized simple polygons (ICALP 2026) rests on a single-port sector decomposition: grid vertices are labelled by the minimum number of bends needed to reach an oriented terminal. We develop the structure theory of this decomposition on grid domains with holes. Once its endpoint convention is made precise, bend distance extends to a canonical piecewise-affine function on the domain, and the sector graph is the parallel-edge shadow of the Reeb multigraph of that function. For a finite pure planar cubical domain with $h$ holes this yields $β_1(Γ_p) \le β_1(R_{f_p}) \le h$, together with a linear-time computable feedback set of at most $h$ sector vertices; coordinate-level one-hole examples show that the finer hole-free geometry -- unique predecessors and straight baselines -- fails. On hole-free cubical disks the machinery is exact: every positive sector is the one-sided extrusion of a unique straight parent interface, and the two-port common refinement has treewidth exactly $c_2^\square=3$, although sector count, cycle rank, and feedback number are unbounded already there. The endpoint precision is necessary rather than cosmetic: under the literal reading of the source's definition, one hole-free unit square has sector graph $C_3$, falsifying the single-port tree lemma; the augmented convention used here is exactly the metric computed by the source's own layering procedure. We do not obtain an $f(k,h)n^{O(1)}$ algorithm; the paper supplies the structural first step and a falsification tool for that program.

</details>


### [131] [Exact Computation of Trait-induced Merge Trees for Bivariate Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07181)
*Petar Hristov,Ingrid Hotz,Talha Bin Masood*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究分段线性双变量场中特征诱导合并树（TIMTs）的精确计算方法。作者发现每个四面体内的受限子水平集是凸的，因此局部合并树结构平凡，全局拓扑变化仅通过单纯形边界粘合产生。基于此观察构建加权图，其合并树与距离场的精确合并树同构，并建立了与Jacobi集的关系及误差上界，实现于CGAL和VTK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIMT计算方法在网格顶点采样距离场并假设分段线性插值，但欧氏距离到特征函数在原始网格上一般不是分段线性的，导致合并树可能遗漏零值特征，并扰动极小值和合并事件的位置与数值。因此需要精确计算TIMT的方法。

Method: 1) 证明每个四面体内的受限子水平集为凸集，从而局部合并树结构平凡，全局拓扑变化仅由单纯形边界的粘合产生；2) 基于该观察构建加权图，其合并树与精确距离场的合并树同构；3) 将TIMT与Jacobi集关联，显示非零边事件的定位由底层双变量映射的奇异结构决定；4) 建立顶点采样线性插值误差的理论上界（以射程中网格边最大投影长度表示）；5) 扩展至线、线段和有限点集特征；6) 使用CGAL和VTK实现。

Result: 提出了精确计算TIMT的算法，避免了近似采样导致的零值特征遗漏和极值/合并事件扰动问题。建立了TIMT与Jacobi集的理论联系，给出了插值误差的理论上界，并在合成和真实数据集上进行了演示。

Conclusion: 通过凸性观察和加权图构造实现了TIMT的精确计算，填补了现有近似方法的理论缺陷，并提供了与Jacobi集的深层联系及误差界分析，为多变量数据的特征级集选择与浏览提供了更精确的拓扑工具。

Abstract: Trait-induced merge trees (TIMTs) provide a robust topology-based method for selecting and browsing feature level sets in multivariate data by analyzing the distance field induced by a user-specified trait in attribute space. Existing TIMT computations typically sample this distance field at mesh vertices and assume piecewise-linear interpolation, although the Euclidean distance-to-trait function is generally not piecewise linear on the original mesh. As a result, the resulting merge tree may miss zero-valued features and may perturb the locations and values of minima and merge events. We study the exact computation of TIMTs for piecewise-linear bivariate fields, focusing first on point traits. We show that the restricted sublevel sets inside each tetrahedron are convex and therefore have trivial local merge-tree structure, implying that global topological changes arise only through gluing across simplex boundaries. Based on this observation, we construct a weighted graph whose merge tree is isomorphic to the exact merge tree of the induced distance field. We further relate TIMTs to Jacobi sets, showing how nonzero edge events of the TIMT are localized by the singular structure of the underlying bivariate map. We establish a theoretical upper bound on the error of the vertex-sampled linear interpolation, expressed in terms of the maximum length of projected mesh edges in the range. We discuss extensions to line, line-segment, and finite point-set traits, and implement the method robustly using CGAL and VTK, demonstrating results on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>
